target datalayout = "e-m:w-p270:32:32-p271:32:32-p272:64:64-i64:64-i128:128-f80:128-n8:16:32:64-S128"
target triple = "x86_64-pc-windows-msvc"
@sollang_runtime_stdout_written = private global i32 0
declare ptr @GetStdHandle(i32)
declare i32 @GetConsoleMode(ptr, ptr)
declare i32 @WriteConsoleW(ptr, ptr, i32, ptr, ptr)
declare i32 @WriteFile(ptr, ptr, i32, ptr, ptr)
declare i32 @MultiByteToWideChar(i32, i32, ptr, i32, ptr, i32)
declare ptr @GetProcessHeap()
declare ptr @HeapAlloc(ptr, i32, i64)
declare i32 @HeapFree(ptr, i32, ptr)
define internal i32 @sollang_runtime_write_stdout_bytes(ptr %handle, ptr %data, i32 %len) {
entry:
  %mode = alloca i32, align 4
  %console_status = call i32 @GetConsoleMode(ptr %handle, ptr %mode)
  %is_console = icmp ne i32 %console_status, 0
  br i1 %is_console, label %console_prepare, label %redirected
redirected:
  %redirected_ok = call i32 @WriteFile(ptr %handle, ptr %data, i32 %len, ptr @sollang_runtime_stdout_written, ptr null)
  ret i32 %redirected_ok
console_prepare:
  %wide_chars = call i32 @MultiByteToWideChar(i32 65001, i32 8, ptr %data, i32 %len, ptr null, i32 0)
  %valid_utf8 = icmp sgt i32 %wide_chars, 0
  br i1 %valid_utf8, label %allocate, label %conversion_failed
allocate:
  %wide_bytes32 = shl i32 %wide_chars, 1
  %wide_bytes = zext i32 %wide_bytes32 to i64
  %heap = call ptr @GetProcessHeap()
  %wide = call ptr @HeapAlloc(ptr %heap, i32 0, i64 %wide_bytes)
  %allocated = icmp ne ptr %wide, null
  br i1 %allocated, label %convert, label %conversion_failed
convert:
  %converted = call i32 @MultiByteToWideChar(i32 65001, i32 8, ptr %data, i32 %len, ptr %wide, i32 %wide_chars)
  %converted_all = icmp eq i32 %converted, %wide_chars
  br i1 %converted_all, label %console_write, label %conversion_free
console_write:
  %console_ok = call i32 @WriteConsoleW(ptr %handle, ptr %wide, i32 %wide_chars, ptr @sollang_runtime_stdout_written, ptr null)
  %freed = call i32 @HeapFree(ptr %heap, i32 0, ptr %wide)
  ret i32 %console_ok
conversion_free:
  %freed_after_failure = call i32 @HeapFree(ptr %heap, i32 0, ptr %wide)
  br label %conversion_failed
conversion_failed:
  store i32 0, ptr @sollang_runtime_stdout_written, align 4
  ret i32 0
}
@sollang_runtime_newline = private constant [1 x i8] c"\0A"
@sollang_runtime_stdout_buffer = private global [1048576 x i8] zeroinitializer
@sollang_runtime_stdout_count = private global i64 0
declare void @llvm.memcpy.p0.p0.i64(ptr, ptr, i64, i1 immarg)
define internal void @sollang_runtime_flush_stdout() {
entry:
  %count = load i64, ptr @sollang_runtime_stdout_count, align 8
  %has_data = icmp ugt i64 %count, 0
  br i1 %has_data, label %write, label %done
write:
  %handle = call ptr @GetStdHandle(i32 -11)
  %count32 = trunc i64 %count to i32
  %ok = call i32 @sollang_runtime_write_stdout_bytes(ptr %handle, ptr @sollang_runtime_stdout_buffer, i32 %count32)
  store i64 0, ptr @sollang_runtime_stdout_count, align 8
  br label %done
done:
  ret void
}
define internal void @sollang_runtime_append_stdout(ptr %data, i64 %len) {
entry:
  %count = load i64, ptr @sollang_runtime_stdout_count, align 8
  %total = add i64 %count, %len
  %fits = icmp ule i64 %total, 1048576
  br i1 %fits, label %append, label %flush
flush:
  call void @sollang_runtime_flush_stdout()
  %large = icmp ugt i64 %len, 1048576
  br i1 %large, label %direct, label %append_empty
direct:
  %handle = call ptr @GetStdHandle(i32 -11)
  %len32 = trunc i64 %len to i32
  %ok = call i32 @sollang_runtime_write_stdout_bytes(ptr %handle, ptr %data, i32 %len32)
  ret void
append_empty:
  call void @llvm.memcpy.p0.p0.i64(ptr @sollang_runtime_stdout_buffer, ptr %data, i64 %len, i1 false)
  store i64 %len, ptr @sollang_runtime_stdout_count, align 8
  ret void
append:
  %slot = getelementptr i8, ptr @sollang_runtime_stdout_buffer, i64 %count
  call void @llvm.memcpy.p0.p0.i64(ptr %slot, ptr %data, i64 %len, i1 false)
  store i64 %total, ptr @sollang_runtime_stdout_count, align 8
  ret void
}
define internal void @sollang_runtime_print(ptr %data, i64 %len, i1 %newline) {
entry:
  call void @sollang_runtime_append_stdout(ptr %data, i64 %len)
  br i1 %newline, label %write_newline, label %done
write_newline:
  call void @sollang_runtime_append_stdout(ptr @sollang_runtime_newline, i64 1)
  br label %done
done:
  ret void
}
define internal void @sollang_runtime_print_i32(i32 %value, i1 %newline) {
entry:
  %buffer = alloca [12 x i8], align 1
  %end = getelementptr [12 x i8], ptr %buffer, i64 0, i64 12
  %wide = sext i32 %value to i64
  %negative = icmp slt i64 %wide, 0
  %negated = sub i64 0, %wide
  %magnitude = select i1 %negative, i64 %negated, i64 %wide
  br label %digits
digits:
  %current = phi i64 [ %magnitude, %entry ], [ %quotient, %digits ]
  %cursor = phi ptr [ %end, %entry ], [ %digit_slot, %digits ]
  %digit = urem i64 %current, 10
  %quotient = udiv i64 %current, 10
  %digit_slot = getelementptr i8, ptr %cursor, i64 -1
  %digit8 = trunc i64 %digit to i8
  %ascii = add i8 %digit8, 48
  store i8 %ascii, ptr %digit_slot, align 1
  %more = icmp ne i64 %quotient, 0
  br i1 %more, label %digits, label %digits_done
digits_done:
  br i1 %negative, label %write_sign, label %emit
write_sign:
  %sign_slot = getelementptr i8, ptr %digit_slot, i64 -1
  store i8 45, ptr %sign_slot, align 1
  br label %emit
emit:
  %start = phi ptr [ %digit_slot, %digits_done ], [ %sign_slot, %write_sign ]
  %end_address = ptrtoint ptr %end to i64
  %start_address = ptrtoint ptr %start to i64
  %length = sub i64 %end_address, %start_address
  call void @sollang_runtime_print(ptr %start, i64 %length, i1 %newline)
  ret void
}
define internal void @sollang_runtime_print_i64(i64 %value, i1 %signed, i1 %newline) {
entry:
  %buffer = alloca [21 x i8], align 1
  %end = getelementptr [21 x i8], ptr %buffer, i64 0, i64 21
  %negative_value = icmp slt i64 %value, 0
  %negative = and i1 %signed, %negative_value
  %negated = sub i64 0, %value
  %magnitude = select i1 %negative, i64 %negated, i64 %value
  br label %digits
digits:
  %current = phi i64 [ %magnitude, %entry ], [ %quotient, %digits ]
  %cursor = phi ptr [ %end, %entry ], [ %digit_slot, %digits ]
  %digit = urem i64 %current, 10
  %quotient = udiv i64 %current, 10
  %digit_slot = getelementptr i8, ptr %cursor, i64 -1
  %digit8 = trunc i64 %digit to i8
  %ascii = add i8 %digit8, 48
  store i8 %ascii, ptr %digit_slot, align 1
  %more = icmp ne i64 %quotient, 0
  br i1 %more, label %digits, label %digits_done
digits_done:
  br i1 %negative, label %write_sign, label %emit
write_sign:
  %sign_slot = getelementptr i8, ptr %digit_slot, i64 -1
  store i8 45, ptr %sign_slot, align 1
  br label %emit
emit:
  %start = phi ptr [ %digit_slot, %digits_done ], [ %sign_slot, %write_sign ]
  %end_address = ptrtoint ptr %end to i64
  %start_address = ptrtoint ptr %start to i64
  %length = sub i64 %end_address, %start_address
  call void @sollang_runtime_print(ptr %start, i64 %length, i1 %newline)
  ret void
}
@sollang_runtime_bool_true = private constant [4 x i8] c"true"
@sollang_runtime_bool_false = private constant [5 x i8] c"false"
define internal void @sollang_runtime_print_i1(i1 %value, i1 %newline) {
entry:
  %data = select i1 %value, ptr @sollang_runtime_bool_true, ptr @sollang_runtime_bool_false
  %length = select i1 %value, i64 4, i64 5
  call void @sollang_runtime_print(ptr %data, i64 %length, i1 %newline)
  ret void
}
declare ptr @malloc(i64)
declare void @free(ptr)
%sollang.dict = type { ptr, ptr, i64, i64 }
define internal void @sollang_drop_t26(%sollang.dict %value) {
entry:
  %keys = extractvalue %sollang.dict %value, 0
  %controls = extractvalue %sollang.dict %value, 1
  %capacity = extractvalue %sollang.dict %value, 3
  call void @free(ptr %controls)
  ret void
}
declare void @llvm.trap()
define void @sollang_m0_s0(ptr %arg_mut_struct) {
entry:
  %arg = load %sollang.dict, ptr %arg_mut_struct, align 8
  %v19 = load %sollang.dict, ptr %arg_mut_struct, align 8
  %set18_source = load %sollang.dict, ptr %arg_mut_struct, align 8
  %set18_keys = extractvalue %sollang.dict %set18_source, 0
  %set18_controls = extractvalue %sollang.dict %set18_source, 1
  %set18_length = extractvalue %sollang.dict %set18_source, 2
  %set18_capacity = extractvalue %sollang.dict %set18_source, 3
  %set18_query_addr = alloca i32, align 4
  store i32 1, ptr %set18_query_addr, align 4
  %set18_query = load i32, ptr %set18_query_addr, align 4
  %set18_query_key64 = sext i32 %set18_query to i64
  %set18_query_hash_high = lshr i64 %set18_query_key64, 32
  %set18_query_hash_fold = xor i64 %set18_query_key64, %set18_query_hash_high
  %set18_query_hash = mul i64 %set18_query_hash_fold, -7046029254386353131
  %set18_h2_shifted = lshr i64 %set18_query_hash, 57
  %set18_h2_raw = and i64 %set18_h2_shifted, 127
  %set18_h2_zero = icmp eq i64 %set18_h2_raw, 0
  %set18_h2_value = select i1 %set18_h2_zero, i64 1, i64 %set18_h2_raw
  %set18_h2 = trunc i64 %set18_h2_value to i8
  %set18_mask = sub i64 %set18_capacity, 1
  %set18_start_slot = and i64 %set18_query_hash, %set18_mask
  br label %set18_lookup_entry
set18_lookup_entry:
  br label %set18_lookup
set18_lookup:
  %set18_slot = phi i64 [ %set18_start_slot, %set18_lookup_entry ], [ %set18_next_slot, %set18_advance ]
  %set18_probe = phi i64 [ 0, %set18_lookup_entry ], [ %set18_next_probe, %set18_advance ]
  %set18_first_deleted = phi i64 [ %set18_capacity, %set18_lookup_entry ], [ %set18_next_first_deleted, %set18_advance ]
  %set18_active = icmp ult i64 %set18_probe, %set18_capacity
  br i1 %set18_active, label %set18_check, label %set18_exhausted
set18_check:
  %set18_control_ptr = getelementptr i8, ptr %set18_controls, i64 %set18_slot
  %set18_control = load i8, ptr %set18_control_ptr, align 1
  %set18_deleted = icmp eq i8 %set18_control, -1
  %set18_without_deleted = icmp eq i64 %set18_first_deleted, %set18_capacity
  %set18_capture_deleted = and i1 %set18_deleted, %set18_without_deleted
  %set18_captured_deleted = select i1 %set18_capture_deleted, i64 %set18_slot, i64 %set18_first_deleted
  %set18_empty = icmp eq i8 %set18_control, 0
  br i1 %set18_empty, label %set18_missing, label %set18_check_h2
set18_check_h2:
  %set18_h2_match = icmp eq i8 %set18_control, %set18_h2
  br i1 %set18_h2_match, label %set18_check_key, label %set18_advance
set18_check_key:
  %set18_stored_ptr = getelementptr i32, ptr %set18_keys, i64 %set18_slot
  %set18_stored = load i32, ptr %set18_stored_ptr, align 4
  %set18_lookup_equal = icmp eq i32 %set18_stored, %set18_query
  br i1 %set18_lookup_equal, label %set18_found, label %set18_advance
set18_advance:
  %set18_slot_plus_one = add i64 %set18_slot, 1
  %set18_next_slot = and i64 %set18_slot_plus_one, %set18_mask
  %set18_next_probe = add i64 %set18_probe, 1
  %set18_next_first_deleted = add i64 %set18_captured_deleted, 0
  br label %set18_lookup
set18_exhausted:
  br label %set18_insert_exhausted
set18_insert_exhausted:
  br label %set18_insert_decide
set18_missing:
  %set18_missing_has_deleted = icmp ne i64 %set18_first_deleted, %set18_capacity
  %set18_missing_slot = select i1 %set18_missing_has_deleted, i64 %set18_first_deleted, i64 %set18_slot
  br label %set18_insert_decide
set18_insert_decide:
  %set18_insert_slot = phi i64 [ %set18_first_deleted, %set18_insert_exhausted ], [ %set18_missing_slot, %set18_missing ]
  %set18_next_length = add i64 %set18_length, 1
  %set18_scaled_length = mul i64 %set18_next_length, 8
  %set18_load_limit = mul i64 %set18_capacity, 7
  %set18_fits = icmp ule i64 %set18_scaled_length, %set18_load_limit
  br i1 %set18_fits, label %set18_insert_current, label %set18_grow
set18_insert_current:
  %set18_insert_key_ptr = getelementptr i32, ptr %set18_keys, i64 %set18_insert_slot
  store i32 %set18_query, ptr %set18_insert_key_ptr, align 4
  %set18_insert_control_ptr = getelementptr i8, ptr %set18_controls, i64 %set18_insert_slot
  store i8 %set18_h2, ptr %set18_insert_control_ptr, align 1
  %set18_current_result = insertvalue %sollang.dict %set18_source, i64 %set18_next_length, 2
  store %sollang.dict %set18_current_result, ptr %arg_mut_struct, align 8
  br label %set18_inserted
set18_grow:
  %set18_grown_capacity = mul i64 %set18_capacity, 2
  br label %set18_grow_rehash_entry
set18_grow_rehash_entry:
  %set18_grow_rehash_control_bytes = add i64 %set18_grown_capacity, 16
  %set18_grow_rehash_key_bytes = mul i64 %set18_grown_capacity, 4
  %set18_grow_rehash_allocation_bytes = add i64 %set18_grow_rehash_control_bytes, %set18_grow_rehash_key_bytes
  %set18_grow_rehash_controls = call ptr @malloc(i64 %set18_grow_rehash_allocation_bytes)
  call void @llvm.memset.p0.i64(ptr %set18_grow_rehash_controls, i8 0, i64 %set18_grow_rehash_control_bytes, i1 false)
  %set18_grow_rehash_keys = getelementptr i8, ptr %set18_grow_rehash_controls, i64 %set18_grow_rehash_control_bytes
  br label %set18_grow_rehash_loop
set18_grow_rehash_loop:
  %set18_grow_rehash_index = phi i64 [ 0, %set18_grow_rehash_entry ], [ %set18_grow_rehash_next_index, %set18_grow_rehash_advance ]
  %set18_grow_rehash_active = icmp ult i64 %set18_grow_rehash_index, %set18_capacity
  br i1 %set18_grow_rehash_active, label %set18_grow_rehash_check, label %set18_grow_rehash_done
set18_grow_rehash_check:
  %set18_grow_rehash_old_control_ptr = getelementptr i8, ptr %set18_controls, i64 %set18_grow_rehash_index
  %set18_grow_rehash_old_control = load i8, ptr %set18_grow_rehash_old_control_ptr, align 1
  %set18_grow_rehash_occupied = icmp sgt i8 %set18_grow_rehash_old_control, 0
  br i1 %set18_grow_rehash_occupied, label %set18_grow_rehash_move, label %set18_grow_rehash_advance
set18_grow_rehash_move:
  %set18_grow_rehash_old_key_ptr = getelementptr i32, ptr %set18_keys, i64 %set18_grow_rehash_index
  %set18_grow_rehash_key = load i32, ptr %set18_grow_rehash_old_key_ptr, align 4
  %set18_grow_rehash_key64 = sext i32 %set18_grow_rehash_key to i64
  %set18_grow_rehash_hash_high = lshr i64 %set18_grow_rehash_key64, 32
  %set18_grow_rehash_hash_fold = xor i64 %set18_grow_rehash_key64, %set18_grow_rehash_hash_high
  %set18_grow_rehash_hash = mul i64 %set18_grow_rehash_hash_fold, -7046029254386353131
  %set18_grow_rehash_h2_shifted = lshr i64 %set18_grow_rehash_hash, 57
  %set18_grow_rehash_h2_raw = and i64 %set18_grow_rehash_h2_shifted, 127
  %set18_grow_rehash_h2_zero = icmp eq i64 %set18_grow_rehash_h2_raw, 0
  %set18_grow_rehash_h2_value = select i1 %set18_grow_rehash_h2_zero, i64 1, i64 %set18_grow_rehash_h2_raw
  %set18_grow_rehash_h2 = trunc i64 %set18_grow_rehash_h2_value to i8
  %set18_grow_rehash_mask = sub i64 %set18_grown_capacity, 1
  %set18_grow_rehash_start_slot = and i64 %set18_grow_rehash_hash, %set18_grow_rehash_mask
  br label %set18_grow_rehash_find
set18_grow_rehash_find:
  %set18_grow_rehash_slot = phi i64 [ %set18_grow_rehash_start_slot, %set18_grow_rehash_move ], [ %set18_grow_rehash_next_slot, %set18_grow_rehash_find_advance ]
  %set18_grow_rehash_control_ptr = getelementptr i8, ptr %set18_grow_rehash_controls, i64 %set18_grow_rehash_slot
  %set18_grow_rehash_control = load i8, ptr %set18_grow_rehash_control_ptr, align 1
  %set18_grow_rehash_empty = icmp eq i8 %set18_grow_rehash_control, 0
  br i1 %set18_grow_rehash_empty, label %set18_grow_rehash_store, label %set18_grow_rehash_find_advance
set18_grow_rehash_find_advance:
  %set18_grow_rehash_slot_plus_one = add i64 %set18_grow_rehash_slot, 1
  %set18_grow_rehash_next_slot = and i64 %set18_grow_rehash_slot_plus_one, %set18_grow_rehash_mask
  br label %set18_grow_rehash_find
set18_grow_rehash_store:
  %set18_grow_rehash_new_key_ptr = getelementptr i32, ptr %set18_grow_rehash_keys, i64 %set18_grow_rehash_slot
  store i32 %set18_grow_rehash_key, ptr %set18_grow_rehash_new_key_ptr, align 4
  store i8 %set18_grow_rehash_h2, ptr %set18_grow_rehash_control_ptr, align 1
  br label %set18_grow_rehash_advance
set18_grow_rehash_advance:
  %set18_grow_rehash_next_index = add i64 %set18_grow_rehash_index, 1
  br label %set18_grow_rehash_loop
set18_grow_rehash_done:
  call void @free(ptr %set18_controls)
  %set18_grow_rehash_result0 = insertvalue %sollang.dict poison, ptr %set18_grow_rehash_keys, 0
  %set18_grow_rehash_result1 = insertvalue %sollang.dict %set18_grow_rehash_result0, ptr %set18_grow_rehash_controls, 1
  %set18_grow_rehash_result2 = insertvalue %sollang.dict %set18_grow_rehash_result1, i64 %set18_length, 2
  %set18_grow_rehash_result = insertvalue %sollang.dict %set18_grow_rehash_result2, i64 %set18_grown_capacity, 3
  %set18_grown_mask = sub i64 %set18_grown_capacity, 1
  %set18_grown_start = and i64 %set18_query_hash, %set18_grown_mask
  br label %set18_grown_find
set18_grown_find:
  %set18_grown_slot = phi i64 [ %set18_grown_start, %set18_grow_rehash_done ], [ %set18_grown_next, %set18_grown_advance ]
  %set18_grown_control_ptr = getelementptr i8, ptr %set18_grow_rehash_controls, i64 %set18_grown_slot
  %set18_grown_control = load i8, ptr %set18_grown_control_ptr, align 1
  %set18_grown_empty = icmp eq i8 %set18_grown_control, 0
  br i1 %set18_grown_empty, label %set18_grown_store, label %set18_grown_advance
set18_grown_advance:
  %set18_grown_plus_one = add i64 %set18_grown_slot, 1
  %set18_grown_next = and i64 %set18_grown_plus_one, %set18_grown_mask
  br label %set18_grown_find
set18_grown_store:
  %set18_grown_key_ptr = getelementptr i32, ptr %set18_grow_rehash_keys, i64 %set18_grown_slot
  store i32 %set18_query, ptr %set18_grown_key_ptr, align 4
  store i8 %set18_h2, ptr %set18_grown_control_ptr, align 1
  %set18_grown_result = insertvalue %sollang.dict %set18_grow_rehash_result, i64 %set18_next_length, 2
  store %sollang.dict %set18_grown_result, ptr %arg_mut_struct, align 8
  br label %set18_inserted
set18_found:
  br label %set18_duplicate
set18_inserted:
  br label %set18_complete
set18_duplicate:
  br label %set18_complete
set18_complete:
  %v18 = phi i1 [ true, %set18_inserted ], [ false, %set18_duplicate ]
  %v22 = load %sollang.dict, ptr %arg_mut_struct, align 8
  %set21_source = load %sollang.dict, ptr %arg_mut_struct, align 8
  %set21_keys = extractvalue %sollang.dict %set21_source, 0
  %set21_controls = extractvalue %sollang.dict %set21_source, 1
  %set21_length = extractvalue %sollang.dict %set21_source, 2
  %set21_capacity = extractvalue %sollang.dict %set21_source, 3
  %set21_query_addr = alloca i32, align 4
  store i32 2, ptr %set21_query_addr, align 4
  %set21_query = load i32, ptr %set21_query_addr, align 4
  %set21_query_key64 = sext i32 %set21_query to i64
  %set21_query_hash_high = lshr i64 %set21_query_key64, 32
  %set21_query_hash_fold = xor i64 %set21_query_key64, %set21_query_hash_high
  %set21_query_hash = mul i64 %set21_query_hash_fold, -7046029254386353131
  %set21_h2_shifted = lshr i64 %set21_query_hash, 57
  %set21_h2_raw = and i64 %set21_h2_shifted, 127
  %set21_h2_zero = icmp eq i64 %set21_h2_raw, 0
  %set21_h2_value = select i1 %set21_h2_zero, i64 1, i64 %set21_h2_raw
  %set21_h2 = trunc i64 %set21_h2_value to i8
  %set21_mask = sub i64 %set21_capacity, 1
  %set21_start_slot = and i64 %set21_query_hash, %set21_mask
  br label %set21_lookup_entry
set21_lookup_entry:
  br label %set21_lookup
set21_lookup:
  %set21_slot = phi i64 [ %set21_start_slot, %set21_lookup_entry ], [ %set21_next_slot, %set21_advance ]
  %set21_probe = phi i64 [ 0, %set21_lookup_entry ], [ %set21_next_probe, %set21_advance ]

  %set27_capacity = extractvalue %sollang.dict %set27_source, 3

  %set27_query_key64 = sext i32 %set27_query to i64
  %set27_query_hash_high = lshr i64 %set27_query_key64, 32
  %set27_query_hash_fold = xor i64 %set27_query_key64, %set27_query_hash_high
  %set27_query_hash = mul i64 %set27_query_hash_fold, -7046029254386353131
  %set27_h2_shifted = lshr i64 %set27_query_hash, 57
  %set27_h2_raw = and i64 %set27_h2_shifted, 127

  %set27_h2_value = select i1 %set27_h2_zero, i64 1, i64 %set27_h2_raw
  %set27_h2 = trunc i64 %set27_h2_value to i8
  %set27_mask = sub i64 %set27_capacity, 1

  %set60_insert_slot = phi i64 [ %set60_first_deleted, %set60_insert_exhausted ], [ %set60_missing_slot, %set60_missing ]
  %set60_next_length = add i64 %set60_length, 1
  %set60_scaled_length = mul i64 %set60_next_length, 8
  %set60_load_limit = mul i64 %set60_capacity, 7
  %set60_fits = icmp ule i64 %set60_scaled_length, %set60_load_limit
  br i1 %set60_fits, label %set60_insert_current, label %set60_grow
set60_insert_current:
  %set60_insert_key_ptr = getelementptr i32, ptr %set60_keys, i64 %set60_insert_slot
  store i32 %set60_query, ptr %set60_insert_key_ptr, align 4
  %set60_insert_control_ptr = getelementptr i8, ptr %set60_controls, i64 %set60_insert_slot
  store i8 %set60_h2, ptr %set60_insert_control_ptr, align 1
  %set60_current_result = insertvalue %sollang.dict %set60_source, i64 %set60_next_length, 2
  store %sollang.dict %set60_current_result, ptr %arg_mut_struct, align 8
  br label %set60_inserted
set60_grow:
  %set60_grown_capacity = mul i64 %set60_capacity, 2
  br label %set60_grow_rehash_entry
set60_grow_rehash_entry:
  %set60_grow_rehash_control_bytes = add i64 %set60_grown_capacity, 16
  %set60_grow_rehash_key_bytes = mul i64 %set60_grown_capacity, 4
  %set60_grow_rehash_allocation_bytes = add i64 %set60_grow_rehash_control_bytes, %set60_grow_rehash_key_bytes
  %set60_grow_rehash_controls = call ptr @malloc(i64 %set60_grow_rehash_allocation_bytes)
  call void @llvm.memset.p0.i64(ptr %set60_grow_rehash_controls, i8 0, i64 %set60_grow_rehash_control_bytes, i1 false)
  %set60_grow_rehash_keys = getelementptr i8, ptr %set60_grow_rehash_controls, i64 %set60_grow_rehash_control_bytes
  br label %set60_grow_rehash_loop
set60_grow_rehash_loop:
  %set60_grow_rehash_index = phi i64 [ 0, %set60_grow_rehash_entry ], [ %set60_grow_rehash_next_index, %set60_grow_rehash_advance ]
  %set60_grow_rehash_active = icmp ult i64 %set60_grow_rehash_index, %set60_capacity
  br i1 %set60_grow_rehash_active, label %set60_grow_rehash_check, label %set60_grow_rehash_done
set60_grow_rehash_check:
  %set60_grow_rehash_old_control_ptr = getelementptr i8, ptr %set60_controls, i64 %set60_grow_rehash_index
  %set60_grow_rehash_old_control = load i8, ptr %set60_grow_rehash_old_control_ptr, align 1
  %set60_grow_rehash_occupied = icmp sgt i8 %set60_grow_rehash_old_control, 0
  br i1 %set60_grow_rehash_occupied, label %set60_grow_rehash_move, label %set60_grow_rehash_advance
set60_grow_rehash_move:
  %set60_grow_rehash_old_key_ptr = getelementptr i32, ptr %set60_keys, i64 %set60_grow_rehash_index
  %set60_grow_rehash_key = load i32, ptr %set60_grow_rehash_old_key_ptr, align 4
  %set60_grow_rehash_key64 = sext i32 %set60_grow_rehash_key to i64
  %set60_grow_rehash_hash_high = lshr i64 %set60_grow_rehash_key64, 32
  %set60_grow_rehash_hash_fold = xor i64 %set60_grow_rehash_key64, %set60_grow_rehash_hash_high
  %set60_grow_rehash_hash = mul i64 %set60_grow_rehash_hash_fold, -7046029254386353131
  %set60_grow_rehash_h2_shifted = lshr i64 %set60_grow_rehash_hash, 57
  %set60_grow_rehash_h2_raw = and i64 %set60_grow_rehash_h2_shifted, 127
  %set60_grow_rehash_h2_zero = icmp eq i64 %set60_grow_rehash_h2_raw, 0
  %set60_grow_rehash_h2_value = select i1 %set60_grow_rehash_h2_zero, i64 1, i64 %set60_grow_rehash_h2_raw
  %set60_grow_rehash_h2 = trunc i64 %set60_grow_rehash_h2_value to i8
  %set60_grow_rehash_mask = sub i64 %set60_grown_capacity, 1
  %set60_grow_rehash_start_slot = and i64 %set60_grow_rehash_hash, %set60_grow_rehash_mask
  br label %set60_grow_rehash_find
set60_grow_rehash_find:
  %set60_grow_rehash_slot = phi i64 [ %set60_grow_rehash_start_slot, %set60_grow_rehash_move ], [ %set60_grow_rehash_next_slot, %set60_grow_rehash_find_advance ]
  %set60_grow_rehash_control_ptr = getelementptr i8, ptr %set60_grow_rehash_controls, i64 %set60_grow_rehash_slot
  %set60_grow_rehash_control = load i8, ptr %set60_grow_rehash_control_ptr, align 1
  %set60_grow_rehash_empty = icmp eq i8 %set60_grow_rehash_control, 0
  br i1 %set60_grow_rehash_empty, label %set60_grow_rehash_store, label %set60_grow_rehash_find_advance
set60_grow_rehash_find_advance:
  %set60_grow_rehash_slot_plus_one = add i64 %set60_grow_rehash_slot, 1
  %set60_grow_rehash_next_slot = and i64 %set60_grow_rehash_slot_plus_one, %set60_grow_rehash_mask
  br label %set60_grow_rehash_find
set60_grow_rehash_store:
  %set60_grow_rehash_new_key_ptr = getelementptr i32, ptr %set60_grow_rehash_keys, i64 %set60_grow_rehash_slot
  store i32 %set60_grow_rehash_key, ptr %set60_grow_rehash_new_key_ptr, align 4
  store i8 %set60_grow_rehash_h2, ptr %set60_grow_rehash_control_ptr, align 1
  br label %set60_grow_rehash_advance
set60_grow_rehash_advance:
  %set60_grow_rehash_next_index = add i64 %set60_grow_rehash_index, 1
  br label %set60_grow_rehash_loop
set60_grow_rehash_done:
  call void @free(ptr %set60_controls)
  %set60_grow_rehash_result0 = insertvalue %sollang.dict poison, ptr %set60_grow_rehash_keys, 0
  %set60_grow_rehash_result1 = insertvalue %sollang.dict %set60_grow_rehash_result0, ptr %set60_grow_rehash_controls, 1
  %set60_grow_rehash_result2 = insertvalue %sollang.dict %set60_grow_rehash_result1, i64 %set60_length, 2
  %set60_grow_rehash_result = insertvalue %sollang.dict %set60_grow_rehash_result2, i64 %set60_grown_capacity, 3
  %set60_grown_mask = sub i64 %set60_grown_capacity, 1
  %set60_grown_start = and i64 %set60_query_hash, %set60_grown_mask
  br label %set60_grown_find
set60_grown_find:
  %set60_grown_slot = phi i64 [ %set60_grown_start, %set60_grow_rehash_done ], [ %set60_grown_next, %set60_grown_advance ]
  %set60_grown_control_ptr = getelementptr i8, ptr %set60_grow_rehash_controls, i64 %set60_grown_slot
  %set60_grown_control = load i8, ptr %set60_grown_control_ptr, align 1
  %set60_grown_empty = icmp eq i8 %set60_grown_control, 0
  br i1 %set60_grown_empty, label %set60_grown_store, label %set60_grown_advance
set60_grown_advance:
  %set60_grown_plus_one = add i64 %set60_grown_slot, 1
  %set60_grown_next = and i64 %set60_grown_plus_one, %set60_grown_mask
  br label %set60_grown_find
set60_grown_store:
  %set60_grown_key_ptr = getelementptr i32, ptr %set60_grow_rehash_keys, i64 %set60_grown_slot
  store i32 %set60_query, ptr %set60_grown_key_ptr, align 4
  store i8 %set60_h2, ptr %set60_grown_control_ptr, align 1
  %set60_grown_result = insertvalue %sollang.dict %set60_grow_rehash_result, i64 %set60_next_length, 2
  store %sollang.dict %set60_grown_result, ptr %arg_mut_struct, align 8
  br label %set60_inserted
set60_found:
  br label %set60_duplicate
set60_inserted:
  br label %set60_complete
set60_duplicate:
  br label %set60_complete
set60_complete:
  %v60 = phi i1 [ true, %set60_inserted ], [ false, %set60_duplicate ]
  ret void
}
define %sollang.dict @sollang_m0_s16() {
entry:
  %v65_requested = add i64 0, 4
  %v65_nonnegative = icmp sge i64 %v65_requested, 0
  br i1 %v65_nonnegative, label %collection65_capacity_ok, label %collection65_capacity_fail
collection65_capacity_fail:
  call void @llvm.trap()
  unreachable
collection65_capacity_ok:
  %v65_scaled = mul i64 %v65_requested, 8
  %v65_rounded_entries = add i64 %v65_scaled, 6
  %v65_required = udiv i64 %v65_rounded_entries, 7
  %v65_below_minimum = icmp ult i64 %v65_required, 16
  %v65_minimum = select i1 %v65_below_minimum, i64 16, i64 %v65_required
  %v65_minus_one = sub i64 %v65_minimum, 1
  %v65_shift1 = lshr i64 %v65_minus_one, 1
  %v65_round1 = or i64 %v65_minus_one, %v65_shift1
  %v65_shift2 = lshr i64 %v65_round1, 2
  %v65_round2 = or i64 %v65_round1, %v65_shift2
  %v65_shift4 = lshr i64 %v65_round2, 4
  %v65_round4 = or i64 %v65_round2, %v65_shift4
  %v65_shift8 = lshr i64 %v65_round4, 8
  %v65_round8 = or i64 %v65_round4, %v65_shift8
  %v65_shift16 = lshr i64 %v65_round8, 16
  %v65_round16 = or i64 %v65_round8, %v65_shift16
  %v65_shift32 = lshr i64 %v65_round16, 32
  %v65_round32 = or i64 %v65_round16, %v65_shift32
  %v65_capacity = add i64 %v65_round32, 1
  %v65_control_bytes = add i64 %v65_capacity, 16
  %v65_key_bytes = mul i64 %v65_capacity, 4
  %v65_bytes = add i64 %v65_control_bytes, %v65_key_bytes
  %v65_controls = call ptr @malloc(i64 %v65_bytes)
  call void @llvm.memset.p0.i64(ptr %v65_controls, i8 0, i64 %v65_control_bytes, i1 false)
  %v65_keys = getelementptr i8, ptr %v65_controls, i64 %v65_control_bytes
  %v65_0 = insertvalue %sollang.dict poison, ptr %v65_keys, 0
  %v65_1 = insertvalue %sollang.dict %v65_0, ptr %v65_controls, 1
  %v65_2 = insertvalue %sollang.dict %v65_1, i64 0, 2
  %v65 = insertvalue %sollang.dict %v65_2, i64 %v65_capacity, 3
  ret %sollang.dict %v65
}
define i32 @sollang_m0_s17() {
entry:
  %slot69 = alloca %sollang.dict, align 8
  %slot72 = alloca i32, align 4
  store i32 0, ptr %slot72, align 4

  br i1 %v74_nonnegative, label %collection74_capacity_ok, label %collection74_capacity_fail

  %v74 = insertvalue %sollang.dict %v74_2, i64 %v74_capacity, 3

  %set76_source = load %sollang.dict, ptr %slot69, align 8

  %set76_controls = extractvalue %sollang.dict %set76_source, 1
  %set76_length = extractvalue %sollang.dict %set76_source, 2
  %set76_capacity = extractvalue %sollang.dict %set76_source, 3
  %set76_query_addr = alloca i32, align 4

  %set76_query_hash_high = lshr i64 %set76_query_key64, 32

  %set76_query_hash = mul i64 %set76_query_hash_fold, -7046029254386353131

set76_lookup_entry:

set76_lookup:

  %set76_first_deleted = phi i64 [ %set76_capacity, %set76_lookup_entry ], [ %set76_next_first_deleted, %set76_advance ]

  %set76_without_deleted = icmp eq i64 %set76_first_deleted, %set76_capacity
  %set76_capture_deleted = and i1 %set76_deleted, %set76_without_deleted
  %set76_captured_deleted = select i1 %set76_capture_deleted, i64 %set76_slot, i64 %set76_first_deleted
  %set76_empty = icmp eq i8 %set76_control, 0
  br i1 %set76_empty, label %set76_missing, label %set76_check_h2
set76_check_h2:
  %set76_h2_match = icmp eq i8 %set76_control, %set76_h2
  br i1 %set76_h2_match, label %set76_check_key, label %set76_advance
set76_check_key:
  %set76_stored_ptr = getelementptr i32, ptr %set76_keys, i64 %set76_slot
  %set76_stored = load i32, ptr %set76_stored_ptr, align 4
  %set76_lookup_equal = icmp eq i32 %set76_stored, %set76_query
  br i1 %set76_lookup_equal, label %set76_found, label %set76_advance
set76_advance:
  %set76_slot_plus_one = add i64 %set76_slot, 1
  %set76_next_slot = and i64 %set76_slot_plus_one, %set76_mask
  %set76_next_probe = add i64 %set76_probe, 1
  %set76_next_first_deleted = add i64 %set76_captured_deleted, 0
  br label %set76_lookup
set76_exhausted:
  br label %set76_insert_exhausted
set76_insert_exhausted:

set76_missing:
  %set76_missing_has_deleted = icmp ne i64 %set76_first_deleted, %set76_capacity
  %set76_missing_slot = select i1 %set76_missing_has_deleted, i64 %set76_first_deleted, i64 %set76_slot

set76_insert_decide:

  %set76_fits = icmp ule i64 %set76_scaled_length, %set76_load_limit

set76_insert_current:

  store i8 %set76_h2, ptr %set76_insert_control_ptr, align 1
  %set76_current_result = insertvalue %sollang.dict %set76_source, i64 %set76_next_length, 2

set76_grow:

set76_grow_rehash_entry:

set76_grow_rehash_loop:

set76_grow_rehash_check:

  %set76_grow_rehash_hash_high = lshr i64 %set76_grow_rehash_key64, 32
  %set76_grow_rehash_hash_fold = xor i64 %set76_grow_rehash_key64, %set76_grow_rehash_hash_high
  %set76_grow_rehash_hash = mul i64 %set76_grow_rehash_hash_fold, -7046029254386353131
  %set76_grow_rehash_h2_shifted = lshr i64 %set76_grow_rehash_hash, 57
  %set76_grow_rehash_h2_raw = and i64 %set76_grow_rehash_h2_shifted, 127
  %set76_grow_rehash_h2_zero = icmp eq i64 %set76_grow_rehash_h2_raw, 0
  %set76_grow_rehash_h2_value = select i1 %set76_grow_rehash_h2_zero, i64 1, i64 %set76_grow_rehash_h2_raw

  %set76_grow_rehash_next_slot = and i64 %set76_grow_rehash_slot_plus_one, %set76_grow_rehash_mask

set76_grow_rehash_advance:

  br label %set76_grow_rehash_loop
set76_grow_rehash_done:
  call void @free(ptr %set76_controls)
  %set76_grow_rehash_result0 = insertvalue %sollang.dict poison, ptr %set76_grow_rehash_keys, 0
  %set76_grow_rehash_result1 = insertvalue %sollang.dict %set76_grow_rehash_result0, ptr %set76_grow_rehash_controls, 1
  %set76_grow_rehash_result2 = insertvalue %sollang.dict %set76_grow_rehash_result1, i64 %set76_length, 2
  %set76_grow_rehash_result = insertvalue %sollang.dict %set76_grow_rehash_result2, i64 %set76_grown_capacity, 3
  %set76_grown_mask = sub i64 %set76_grown_capacity, 1
  %set76_grown_start = and i64 %set76_query_hash, %set76_grown_mask
  br label %set76_grown_find
set76_grown_find:
  %set76_grown_slot = phi i64 [ %set76_grown_start, %set76_grow_rehash_done ], [ %set76_grown_next, %set76_grown_advance ]
  %set76_grown_control_ptr = getelementptr i8, ptr %set76_grow_rehash_controls, i64 %set76_grown_slot
  %set76_grown_control = load i8, ptr %set76_grown_control_ptr, align 1
  %set76_grown_empty = icmp eq i8 %set76_grown_control, 0
  br i1 %set76_grown_empty, label %set76_grown_store, label %set76_grown_advance
set76_grown_advance:
  %set76_grown_plus_one = add i64 %set76_grown_slot, 1
  %set76_grown_next = and i64 %set76_grown_plus_one, %set76_grown_mask
  br label %set76_grown_find
set76_grown_store:
  %set76_grown_key_ptr = getelementptr i32, ptr %set76_grow_rehash_keys, i64 %set76_grown_slot
  store i32 %set76_query, ptr %set76_grown_key_ptr, align 4
  store i8 %set76_h2, ptr %set76_grown_control_ptr, align 1
  %set76_grown_result = insertvalue %sollang.dict %set76_grow_rehash_result, i64 %set76_next_length, 2
  store %sollang.dict %set76_grown_result, ptr %slot69, align 8
  br label %set76_inserted
set76_found:
  br label %set76_duplicate
set76_inserted:
  br label %set76_complete
set76_duplicate:
  br label %set76_complete
set76_complete:
  %v76 = phi i1 [ true, %set76_inserted ], [ false, %set76_duplicate ]
  %v80 = load %sollang.dict, ptr %slot69, align 8
  %set79_source = load %sollang.dict, ptr %slot69, align 8
  %set79_keys = extractvalue %sollang.dict %set79_source, 0
  %set79_controls = extractvalue %sollang.dict %set79_source, 1
  %set79_length = extractvalue %sollang.dict %set79_source, 2
  %set79_capacity = extractvalue %sollang.dict %set79_source, 3
  %set79_query_addr = alloca i32, align 4
  store i32 2, ptr %set79_query_addr, align 4
  %set79_query = load i32, ptr %set79_query_addr, align 4
  %set79_query_key64 = sext i32 %set79_query to i64
  %set79_query_hash_high = lshr i64 %set79_query_key64, 32
  %set79_query_hash_fold = xor i64 %set79_query_key64, %set79_query_hash_high
  %set79_query_hash = mul i64 %set79_query_hash_fold, -7046029254386353131
  %set79_h2_shifted = lshr i64 %set79_query_hash, 57
  %set79_h2_raw = and i64 %set79_h2_shifted, 127
  %set79_h2_zero = icmp eq i64 %set79_h2_raw, 0
  %set79_h2_value = select i1 %set79_h2_zero, i64 1, i64 %set79_h2_raw
  %set79_h2 = trunc i64 %set79_h2_value to i8
  %set79_mask = sub i64 %set79_capacity, 1
  %set79_start_slot = and i64 %set79_query_hash, %set79_mask
  br label %set79_lookup_entry
set79_lookup_entry:
  br label %set79_lookup
set79_lookup:
  %set79_slot = phi i64 [ %set79_start_slot, %set79_lookup_entry ], [ %set79_next_slot, %set79_advance ]
  %set79_probe = phi i64 [ 0, %set79_lookup_entry ], [ %set79_next_probe, %set79_advance ]
  %set79_first_deleted = phi i64 [ %set79_capacity, %set79_lookup_entry ], [ %set79_next_first_deleted, %set79_advance ]
  %set79_active = icmp ult i64 %set79_probe, %set79_capacity
  br i1 %set79_active, label %set79_check, label %set79_exhausted
set79_check:
  %set79_control_ptr = getelementptr i8, ptr %set79_controls, i64 %set79_slot
  %set79_control = load i8, ptr %set79_control_ptr, align 1
  %set79_deleted = icmp eq i8 %set79_control, -1
  %set79_without_deleted = icmp eq i64 %set79_first_deleted, %set79_capacity
  %set79_capture_deleted = and i1 %set79_deleted, %set79_without_deleted
  %set79_captured_deleted = select i1 %set79_capture_deleted, i64 %set79_slot, i64 %set79_first_deleted
  %set79_empty = icmp eq i8 %set79_control, 0
  br i1 %set79_empty, label %set79_missing, label %set79_check_h2
set79_check_h2:
  %set79_h2_match = icmp eq i8 %set79_control, %set79_h2
  br i1 %set79_h2_match, label %set79_check_key, label %set79_advance
set79_check_key:
  %set79_stored_ptr = getelementptr i32, ptr %set79_keys, i64 %set79_slot
  %set79_stored = load i32, ptr %set79_stored_ptr, align 4
  %set79_lookup_equal = icmp eq i32 %set79_stored, %set79_query
  br i1 %set79_lookup_equal, label %set79_found, label %set79_advance
set79_advance:
  %set79_slot_plus_one = add i64 %set79_slot, 1
  %set79_next_slot = and i64 %set79_slot_plus_one, %set79_mask
  %set79_next_probe = add i64 %set79_probe, 1
  %set79_next_first_deleted = add i64 %set79_captured_deleted, 0
  br label %set79_lookup
set79_exhausted:
  br label %set79_insert_exhausted
set79_insert_exhausted:
  br label %set79_insert_decide
set79_missing:
  %set79_missing_has_deleted = icmp ne i64 %set79_first_deleted, %set79_capacity
  %set79_missing_slot = select i1 %set79_missing_has_deleted, i64 %set79_first_deleted, i64 %set79_slot
  br label %set79_insert_decide
set79_insert_decide:
  %set79_insert_slot = phi i64 [ %set79_first_deleted, %set79_insert_exhausted ], [ %set79_missing_slot, %set79_missing ]
  %set79_next_length = add i64 %set79_length, 1
  %set79_scaled_length = mul i64 %set79_next_length, 8
  %set79_load_limit = mul i64 %set79_capacity, 7
  %set79_fits = icmp ule i64 %set79_scaled_length, %set79_load_limit
  br i1 %set79_fits, label %set79_insert_current, label %set79_grow
set79_insert_current:
  %set79_insert_key_ptr = getelementptr i32, ptr %set79_keys, i64 %set79_insert_slot
  store i32 %set79_query, ptr %set79_insert_key_ptr, align 4
  %set79_insert_control_ptr = getelementptr i8, ptr %set79_controls, i64 %set79_insert_slot
  store i8 %set79_h2, ptr %set79_insert_control_ptr, align 1
  %set79_current_result = insertvalue %sollang.dict %set79_source, i64 %set79_next_length, 2
  store %sollang.dict %set79_current_result, ptr %slot69, align 8
  br label %set79_inserted
set79_grow:
  %set79_grown_capacity = mul i64 %set79_capacity, 2
  br label %set79_grow_rehash_entry
set79_grow_rehash_entry:
  %set79_grow_rehash_control_bytes = add i64 %set79_grown_capacity, 16
  %set79_grow_rehash_key_bytes = mul i64 %set79_grown_capacity, 4
  %set79_grow_rehash_allocation_bytes = add i64 %set79_grow_rehash_control_bytes, %set79_grow_rehash_key_bytes
  %set79_grow_rehash_controls = call ptr @malloc(i64 %set79_grow_rehash_allocation_bytes)
  call void @llvm.memset.p0.i64(ptr %set79_grow_rehash_controls, i8 0, i64 %set79_grow_rehash_control_bytes, i1 false)
  %set79_grow_rehash_keys = getelementptr i8, ptr %set79_grow_rehash_controls, i64 %set79_grow_rehash_control_bytes
  br label %set79_grow_rehash_loop
set79_grow_rehash_loop:
  %set79_grow_rehash_index = phi i64 [ 0, %set79_grow_rehash_entry ], [ %set79_grow_rehash_next_index, %set79_grow_rehash_advance ]
  %set79_grow_rehash_active = icmp ult i64 %set79_grow_rehash_index, %set79_capacity
  br i1 %set79_grow_rehash_active, label %set79_grow_rehash_check, label %set79_grow_rehash_done
set79_grow_rehash_check:
  %set79_grow_rehash_old_control_ptr = getelementptr i8, ptr %set79_controls, i64 %set79_grow_rehash_index
  %set79_grow_rehash_old_control = load i8, ptr %set79_grow_rehash_old_control_ptr, align 1
  %set79_grow_rehash_occupied = icmp sgt i8 %set79_grow_rehash_old_control, 0
  br i1 %set79_grow_rehash_occupied, label %set79_grow_rehash_move, label %set79_grow_rehash_advance
set79_grow_rehash_move:
  %set79_grow_rehash_old_key_ptr = getelementptr i32, ptr %set79_keys, i64 %set79_grow_rehash_index
  %set79_grow_rehash_key = load i32, ptr %set79_grow_rehash_old_key_ptr, align 4
  %set79_grow_rehash_key64 = sext i32 %set79_grow_rehash_key to i64
  %set79_grow_rehash_hash_high = lshr i64 %set79_grow_rehash_key64, 32
  %set79_grow_rehash_hash_fold = xor i64 %set79_grow_rehash_key64, %set79_grow_rehash_hash_high
  %set79_grow_rehash_hash = mul i64 %set79_grow_rehash_hash_fold, -7046029254386353131
  %set79_grow_rehash_h2_shifted = lshr i64 %set79_grow_rehash_hash, 57
  %set79_grow_rehash_h2_raw = and i64 %set79_grow_rehash_h2_shifted, 127
  %set79_grow_rehash_h2_zero = icmp eq i64 %set79_grow_rehash_h2_raw, 0
  %set79_grow_rehash_h2_value = select i1 %set79_grow_rehash_h2_zero, i64 1, i64 %set79_grow_rehash_h2_raw
  %set79_grow_rehash_h2 = trunc i64 %set79_grow_rehash_h2_value to i8
  %set79_grow_rehash_mask = sub i64 %set79_grown_capacity, 1
  %set79_grow_rehash_start_slot = and i64 %set79_grow_rehash_hash, %set79_grow_rehash_mask
  br label %set79_grow_rehash_find
set79_grow_rehash_find:
  %set79_grow_rehash_slot = phi i64 [ %set79_grow_rehash_start_slot, %set79_grow_rehash_move ], [ %set79_grow_rehash_next_slot, %set79_grow_rehash_find_advance ]
  %set79_grow_rehash_control_ptr = getelementptr i8, ptr %set79_grow_rehash_controls, i64 %set79_grow_rehash_slot
  %set79_grow_rehash_control = load i8, ptr %set79_grow_rehash_control_ptr, align 1
  %set79_grow_rehash_empty = icmp eq i8 %set79_grow_rehash_control, 0
  br i1 %set79_grow_rehash_empty, label %set79_grow_rehash_store, label %set79_grow_rehash_find_advance
set79_grow_rehash_find_advance:
  %set79_grow_rehash_slot_plus_one = add i64 %set79_grow_rehash_slot, 1
  %set79_grow_rehash_next_slot = and i64 %set79_grow_rehash_slot_plus_one, %set79_grow_rehash_mask
  br label %set79_grow_rehash_find
set79_grow_rehash_store:
  %set79_grow_rehash_new_key_ptr = getelementptr i32, ptr %set79_grow_rehash_keys, i64 %set79_grow_rehash_slot
  store i32 %set79_grow_rehash_key, ptr %set79_grow_rehash_new_key_ptr, align 4
  store i8 %set79_grow_rehash_h2, ptr %set79_grow_rehash_control_ptr, align 1
  br label %set79_grow_rehash_advance
set79_grow_rehash_advance:
  %set79_grow_rehash_next_index = add i64 %set79_grow_rehash_index, 1
  br label %set79_grow_rehash_loop
set79_grow_rehash_done:
  call void @free(ptr %set79_controls)
  %set79_grow_rehash_result0 = insertvalue %sollang.dict poison, ptr %set79_grow_rehash_keys, 0
  %set79_grow_rehash_result1 = insertvalue %sollang.dict %set79_grow_rehash_result0, ptr %set79_grow_rehash_controls, 1
  %set79_grow_rehash_result2 = insertvalue %sollang.dict %set79_grow_rehash_result1, i64 %set79_length, 2
  %set79_grow_rehash_result = insertvalue %sollang.dict %set79_grow_rehash_result2, i64 %set79_grown_capacity, 3
  %set79_grown_mask = sub i64 %set79_grown_capacity, 1
  %set79_grown_start = and i64 %set79_query_hash, %set79_grown_mask
  br label %set79_grown_find
set79_grown_find:
  %set79_grown_slot = phi i64 [ %set79_grown_start, %set79_grow_rehash_done ], [ %set79_grown_next, %set79_grown_advance ]
  %set79_grown_control_ptr = getelementptr i8, ptr %set79_grow_rehash_controls, i64 %set79_grown_slot
  %set79_grown_control = load i8, ptr %set79_grown_control_ptr, align 1
  %set79_grown_empty = icmp eq i8 %set79_grown_control, 0
  br i1 %set79_grown_empty, label %set79_grown_store, label %set79_grown_advance
set79_grown_advance:
  %set79_grown_plus_one = add i64 %set79_grown_slot, 1
  %set79_grown_next = and i64 %set79_grown_plus_one, %set79_grown_mask
  br label %set79_grown_find
set79_grown_store:
  %set79_grown_key_ptr = getelementptr i32, ptr %set79_grow_rehash_keys, i64 %set79_grown_slot
  store i32 %set79_query, ptr %set79_grown_key_ptr, align 4
  store i8 %set79_h2, ptr %set79_grown_control_ptr, align 1
  %set79_grown_result = insertvalue %sollang.dict %set79_grow_rehash_result, i64 %set79_next_length, 2
  store %sollang.dict %set79_grown_result, ptr %slot69, align 8
  br label %set79_inserted
set79_found:
  br label %set79_duplicate
set79_inserted:
  br label %set79_complete
set79_duplicate:
  br label %set79_complete
set79_complete:
  %v79 = phi i1 [ true, %set79_inserted ], [ false, %set79_duplicate ]
  %v84 = load %sollang.dict, ptr %slot69, align 8
  %each83_keys = extractvalue %sollang.dict %v84, 0
  %each83_controls = extractvalue %sollang.dict %v84, 1
  %each83_length = extractvalue %sollang.dict %v84, 3
  %each83_index_slot = alloca i64, align 8
  store i64 0, ptr %each83_index_slot, align 8
  br label %each83_header
each83_header:
  %each83_index = load i64, ptr %each83_index_slot, align 8
  %each83_done = icmp eq i64 %each83_index, %each83_length
  br i1 %each83_done, label %each83_exit, label %each83_check
each83_check:
  %each83_control_ptr = getelementptr i8, ptr %each83_controls, i64 %each83_index
  %each83_control = load i8, ptr %each83_control_ptr, align 1
  %each83_occupied = icmp sgt i8 %each83_control, 0
  br i1 %each83_occupied, label %each83_body, label %each83_continue
each83_body:
  %each83_item_ptr = getelementptr i32, ptr %each83_keys, i64 %each83_index
  %each83_item = load i32, ptr %each83_item_ptr, align 4
  %v87 = freeze i32 %each83_item
  %v86 = load i32, ptr %slot72, align 4
  %v85 = add i32 %v86, %v87
  store i32 %v85, ptr %slot72, align 4
  br label %each83_continue
each83_continue:
  %each83_next = add i64 %each83_index, 1
  store i64 %each83_next, ptr %each83_index_slot, align 8
  br label %each83_header
each83_exit:
  %v88 = load i32, ptr %slot72, align 4
  %drop69_current = load %sollang.dict, ptr %slot69, align 8
  call void @sollang_drop_t26(%sollang.dict %drop69_current)
  ret i32 %v88
}
define i32 @main() {
entry:

  %v115 = call i32 @sollang_m0_s17()

each107_body:

  %v110 = load i32, ptr %slot91, align 4

each107_continue:

  store i64 %each107_next, ptr %each107_index_slot, align 8
  br label %each107_header
each107_exit:
  %v113 = load i32, ptr %slot91, align 4
  %v117 = load %sollang.dict, ptr %slot90, align 8
  %set181_source = load %sollang.dict, ptr %slot90, align 8

  %set181_length = extractvalue %sollang.dict %set181_source, 2
  %set181_capacity = extractvalue %sollang.dict %set181_source, 3

  %set181_query_key64 = sext i32 %set181_query to i64
  %set181_query_hash_high = lshr i64 %set181_query_key64, 32

  %set181_query_hash = mul i64 %set181_query_hash_fold, -7046029254386353131

  %set181_h2_raw = and i64 %set181_h2_shifted, 127

  %set181_h2 = trunc i64 %set181_h2_value to i8
  %set181_mask = sub i64 %set181_capacity, 1
  %set181_start_slot = and i64 %set181_query_hash, %set181_mask
  br label %set181_lookup_entry
set181_lookup_entry:
  br label %set181_lookup
set181_lookup:
  %set181_slot = phi i64 [ %set181_start_slot, %set181_lookup_entry ], [ %set181_next_slot, %set181_advance ]
  %set181_probe = phi i64 [ 0, %set181_lookup_entry ], [ %set181_next_probe, %set181_advance ]
  %set181_first_deleted = phi i64 [ %set181_capacity, %set181_lookup_entry ], [ %set181_next_first_deleted, %set181_advance ]
  %set181_active = icmp ult i64 %set181_probe, %set181_capacity

  %set181_capture_deleted = and i1 %set181_deleted, %set181_without_deleted
  %set181_captured_deleted = select i1 %set181_capture_deleted, i64 %set181_slot, i64 %set181_first_deleted
  %set181_empty = icmp eq i8 %set181_control, 0
  br i1 %set181_empty, label %set181_missing, label %set181_check_h2
set181_check_h2:
  %set181_h2_match = icmp eq i8 %set181_control, %set181_h2
  br i1 %set181_h2_match, label %set181_check_key, label %set181_advance
set181_check_key:

  %set181_stored = load i32, ptr %set181_stored_ptr, align 4
  %set181_lookup_equal = icmp eq i32 %set181_stored, %set181_query
  br i1 %set181_lookup_equal, label %set181_found, label %set181_advance
set181_advance:

  %set181_next_slot = and i64 %set181_slot_plus_one, %set181_mask
  %set181_next_probe = add i64 %set181_probe, 1
  %set181_next_first_deleted = add i64 %set181_captured_deleted, 0
  br label %set181_lookup
set181_exhausted:

set181_missing:

set181_found:

set181_present:
  br label %set181_complete
set181_not_found:

set181_complete:

  call void @sollang_runtime_print_i32(i32 %v115, i1 true)
  call void @sollang_runtime_print_i1(i1 %v181, i1 true)
  %v120 = load %sollang.dict, ptr %slot90, align 8
  %set182_source = load %sollang.dict, ptr %slot90, align 8

  %set182_controls = extractvalue %sollang.dict %set182_source, 1
  %set182_length = extractvalue %sollang.dict %set182_source, 2
  %set182_capacity = extractvalue %sollang.dict %set182_source, 3
  %set182_query_addr = alloca i32, align 4
  store i32 99, ptr %set182_query_addr, align 4

  %set191_compact_rehash_active = icmp ult i64 %set191_compact_rehash_index, %set191_capacity
  br i1 %set191_compact_rehash_active, label %set191_compact_rehash_check, label %set191_compact_rehash_done
set191_compact_rehash_check:
  %set191_compact_rehash_old_control_ptr = getelementptr i8, ptr %set191_controls, i64 %set191_compact_rehash_index
  %set191_compact_rehash_old_control = load i8, ptr %set191_compact_rehash_old_control_ptr, align 1
  %set191_compact_rehash_occupied = icmp sgt i8 %set191_compact_rehash_old_control, 0
  br i1 %set191_compact_rehash_occupied, label %set191_compact_rehash_move, label %set191_compact_rehash_advance
set191_compact_rehash_move:
  %set191_compact_rehash_old_key_ptr = getelementptr i32, ptr %set191_keys, i64 %set191_compact_rehash_index
  %set191_compact_rehash_key = load i32, ptr %set191_compact_rehash_old_key_ptr, align 4
  %set191_compact_rehash_key64 = sext i32 %set191_compact_rehash_key to i64
  %set191_compact_rehash_hash_high = lshr i64 %set191_compact_rehash_key64, 32
  %set191_compact_rehash_hash_fold = xor i64 %set191_compact_rehash_key64, %set191_compact_rehash_hash_high
  %set191_compact_rehash_hash = mul i64 %set191_compact_rehash_hash_fold, -7046029254386353131
  %set191_compact_rehash_h2_shifted = lshr i64 %set191_compact_rehash_hash, 57
  %set191_compact_rehash_h2_raw = and i64 %set191_compact_rehash_h2_shifted, 127
  %set191_compact_rehash_h2_zero = icmp eq i64 %set191_compact_rehash_h2_raw, 0
  %set191_compact_rehash_h2_value = select i1 %set191_compact_rehash_h2_zero, i64 1, i64 %set191_compact_rehash_h2_raw
  %set191_compact_rehash_h2 = trunc i64 %set191_compact_rehash_h2_value to i8
  %set191_compact_rehash_mask = sub i64 %set191_capacity, 1
  %set191_compact_rehash_start_slot = and i64 %set191_compact_rehash_hash, %set191_compact_rehash_mask
  br label %set191_compact_rehash_find
set191_compact_rehash_find:
  %set191_compact_rehash_slot = phi i64 [ %set191_compact_rehash_start_slot, %set191_compact_rehash_move ], [ %set191_compact_rehash_next_slot, %set191_compact_rehash_find_advance ]
  %set191_compact_rehash_control_ptr = getelementptr i8, ptr %set191_compact_rehash_controls, i64 %set191_compact_rehash_slot
  %set191_compact_rehash_control = load i8, ptr %set191_compact_rehash_control_ptr, align 1
  %set191_compact_rehash_empty = icmp eq i8 %set191_compact_rehash_control, 0
  br i1 %set191_compact_rehash_empty, label %set191_compact_rehash_store, label %set191_compact_rehash_find_advance
set191_compact_rehash_find_advance:
  %set191_compact_rehash_slot_plus_one = add i64 %set191_compact_rehash_slot, 1
  %set191_compact_rehash_next_slot = and i64 %set191_compact_rehash_slot_plus_one, %set191_compact_rehash_mask
  br label %set191_compact_rehash_find
set191_compact_rehash_store:
  %set191_compact_rehash_new_key_ptr = getelementptr i32, ptr %set191_compact_rehash_keys, i64 %set191_compact_rehash_slot
  store i32 %set191_compact_rehash_key, ptr %set191_compact_rehash_new_key_ptr, align 4
  store i8 %set191_compact_rehash_h2, ptr %set191_compact_rehash_control_ptr, align 1
  br label %set191_compact_rehash_advance
set191_compact_rehash_advance:
  %set191_compact_rehash_next_index = add i64 %set191_compact_rehash_index, 1
  br label %set191_compact_rehash_loop
set191_compact_rehash_done:
  call void @free(ptr %set191_controls)
  %set191_compact_rehash_result0 = insertvalue %sollang.dict poison, ptr %set191_compact_rehash_keys, 0
  %set191_compact_rehash_result1 = insertvalue %sollang.dict %set191_compact_rehash_result0, ptr %set191_compact_rehash_controls, 1
  %set191_compact_rehash_result2 = insertvalue %sollang.dict %set191_compact_rehash_result1, i64 %set191_next_length, 2
  %set191_compact_rehash_result = insertvalue %sollang.dict %set191_compact_rehash_result2, i64 %set191_capacity, 3
  store %sollang.dict %set191_compact_rehash_result, ptr %slot90, align 8
  br label %set191_removed
set191_removed:
  br label %set191_complete
set191_not_found:
  br label %set191_complete
set191_complete:
  %v191 = phi i1 [ true, %set191_removed ], [ false, %set191_not_found ]
  call void @sollang_runtime_print_i1(i1 %v191, i1 true)
  %v178 = load %sollang.dict, ptr %slot90, align 8
  %v180 = load %sollang.dict, ptr %slot90, align 8
  %v192_length_source = load %sollang.dict, ptr %slot90, align 8
  %v192_raw = extractvalue %sollang.dict %v192_length_source, 2
  %v192 = trunc i64 %v192_raw to i32
  %v193_capacity_source = load %sollang.dict, ptr %slot90, align 8
  %v193 = extractvalue %sollang.dict %v193_capacity_source, 3
  call void @sollang_runtime_print_i32(i32 %v192, i1 true)
  call void @sollang_runtime_print_i64(i64 %v193, i1 false, i1 true)
  call void @sollang_runtime_flush_stdout()
  ret i32 0
}
